# 项目: schema_semantic_constrained_ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-16980
- SSH 连接命令: ssh westd-16980  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：接管 DocRED Beam Sweep — 检查现有进程 + 补跑 + 评估

前一个 Worker 在 westd-21461 GPU0 上后台启动了 DocRED beam sweep 进程就退出了。你需要接管：检查哪些已完成、哪些在跑、哪些缺失，然后补跑缺失的并评估。

### 目标
Qwen3-8B LoRA DocRED, k=2 和 k=8, unc + constrained (full_s1), 200-doc subset。共 4 个 config。
Registry 实验 ID: d066_docred_beam_sweep

### 步骤

1. **检查现有状态**（ssh_read_only）：
   ```bash
   # 检查 GPU0 上是否有推理进程在跑
   nvidia-smi
   ps aux | grep -E "run_constrained|beam|docred" | grep -v grep
   
   # 检查是否有 tmux session
   tmux ls 2>/dev/null
   
   # 检查已有输出文件
   ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/d066*docred* 2>/dev/null
   ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/*docred*beam* 2>/dev/null
   
   # 检查 nohup log
   ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/d066*log* /tmp/d066*log* 2>/dev/null
   cat /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/d066*.log 2>/dev/null | tail -20
   ```

2. **判断状态**：
   - 如果有 python 进程在跑推理 → 等待完成（用 tail -f log 或轮询）
   - 如果输出文件已经存在且完整（200 行 JSONL）→ 跳过推理直接评估
   - 如果没有进程、文件不完整或不存在 → 重新运行

3. **如果需要重跑**（ssh_execute）：
   
   先找到正确的命令格式。查看 d066 SciERC 脚本和推理脚本：
   ```bash
   cat /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/scripts/d066_beam_sweep_scierc.sh
   python /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/run_constrained_extraction.py --help 2>/dev/null | head -40
   ```
   
   然后参照 SciERC 脚本格式，**同步运行**（不要 nohup / 不要后台）：
   
   环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
   工作目录：`cd /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie`
   GPU：`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0`
   
   需要跑的 config（跳过已有完整输出的）：
   a) k=2 unc → `/root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/d066_docred_beam2_unc.jsonl`
   b) k=2 con → `/root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/d066_docred_beam2_s1.jsonl`
   c) k=8 unc → `/root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/d066_docred_beam8_unc.jsonl`
   d) k=8 con → `/root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/d066_docred_beam8_s1.jsonl`
   
   关键参数：
   - `--max_new_tokens 4096`
   - `--dataset docred`
   - `--data_path data/docred/dev.json`
   - 200 docs：如果脚本有 `--max_samples` 或 `--num_docs` 参数用它；否则先用 python 截取前 200 个 doc 到临时文件 `/root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/docred_dev_200.json`
   - checkpoint：查找 Qwen DocRED LoRA merged，可能在 `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/` 或 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/` 下

4. **评估所有输出**（ssh_execute）：
   ```bash
   # 用 eval_d064.py 评估每个文件
   # gold_file 需要与推理时用的数据文件匹配（200 docs 的那个）
   python scripts/eval_d064.py --pred_file &lt;file> --gold_file &lt;gold_200_docs> --dataset docred
   ```
   
   收集每个 config：ET%, RV%, DR%, CF%, E-P, E-R, E-F1, R-P, R-R, R-F1, PF%

5. **写报告**（本地）：
   写入 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_6/projects/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/artifacts/_project/report/d066_docred_beam_sweep_eval.md`
   
   包含：
   - k=2, k=8 的完整指标表
   - 与 k=1 (greedy, w5 registry) 和 k=4 (beam-4, d064 registry) 对比
   - Pareto 分析
   - 与 SciERC pattern 的对比（k=2 是否也是 sweet spot）

6. **更新 registry**：
   用 update_registry 更新 d066_docred_beam_sweep 的 metrics 和 notes

### 重要
- 只用 GPU0 (CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0)，GPU1 有另一个实验在跑
- 每个 config 同步等待完成，不要后台运行。DocRED 200 docs 每个 config 约 30-60 min
- 如果 checkpoint 不在 21461 上，报告这个问题而不是尝试下载

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/bin/bash\\n# d066: Beam Width Sweep - DocRED 200-doc subset (westd-16980)\\n# Only k=2,8 (k=1,4 reuse d064 full data)\\n# GPU0: Llama-3.1-8B LoRA DocRED, beam k=2,8, unconstrained + constrained\\n# GPU1: Qwen3-8B LoRA DocRED, beam k=2,8, unconstrained + constrained\\n# Usage: bash scripts/d066_beam_sweep_docred.sh\\nset -euo pipefail\\n\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh\\nconda activate base\\ncd /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie\\n\\nOUTDIR=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/d066_beam_sweep\\\"\\nVIOL_DIR=\\\"${OUTDIR}/violations\\\"\\nmkdir -p \\\"$OUTDIR\\\" \\\"$VIOL_DIR\\\"\\n\\nLLAMA_MODEL=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/llama_lora_docred_merged\\\"\\nQWEN_MODEL=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/qwen_lora_docred_merged\\\"\\nDATA_PATH=\\\"data/docred/dev.json\\\"\\nCONSTRAINTS_PATH=\\\"data/docred/ontology_constraints_docred.json\\\"\\nMAX_NEW_TOKENS=4096\\nMAX_DOCS=200\\nBEAMS=(2 8)\\n\\nrun_inference() {\\n    local gpu=$1 model_path=$2 model_name=$3 beam=$4 mode=$5\\n    local out_name=\\\"${model_name}_docred_200_beam${beam}_${mode}\\\"\\n    local out_file=\\\"${OUTDIR}/${out_name}.jsonl\\\"\\n\\n    if [ -f \\\"$out_file\\\" ]; then\\n        local n_lines=$(wc -l < \\\"$out_file\\\")\\n        if [ \\\"$n_lines\\\" -ge $MAX_DOCS ]; then\\n            echo \\\"$(date): SKIP ${out_name} (already complete: ${n_lines} lines)\\\"\\n            return 0\\n        fi\\n    fi\\n\\n    echo \\\"$(date): START ${out_name} on GPU${gpu}\\\"\\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=$gpu python run_constrained_extraction.py \\\\\\n        --mode \\\"$mode\\\" \\\\\\n        --dataset docred \\\\\\n        --model_path \\\"$model_path\\\" \\\\\\n        --data_path \\\"$DATA_PATH\\\" \\\\\\n        --constraints_path \\\"$CONSTRAINTS_PATH\\\" \\\\\\n        --output_path \\\"$out_file\\\" \\\\\\n        --device cuda:0 \\\\\\n        --max_new_tokens $MAX_NEW_TOKENS \\\\\\n        --max_docs $MAX_DOCS \\\\\\n        --seed 42 \\\\\\n        --decoding_strategy beam \\\\\\n        --num_beams \\\"$beam\\\" \\\\\\n        --skip_on_error\\n    echo \\\"$(date): DONE ${out_name}\\\"\\n}\\n\\nrun_violation_check() {\\n    local model_name=$1 beam=$2 mode=$3\\n    local out_name=\\\"${model_name}_docred_200_beam${beam}_${mode}\\\"\\n    local in_file=\\\"${OUTDIR}/${out_name}.jsonl\\\"\\n    local viol_file=\\\"${VIOL_DIR}/${out_name}_violations.json\\\"\\n\\n    if [ ! -f \\\"$in_file\\\" ]; if not m:\\n        return None\\n    return {\\n        'model': m.group(1),\\n        'dataset': m.group(2),\\n        'beam': int(m.group(3)),\\n        'mode': m.group(4),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef analyze_latency(records):\\n    times = [r['inference_time_seconds'] for r in records if 'inference_time_seconds' in r]\\n    if not times:\\n        return {}\\n    return {\\n        'n_samples': len(times),\\n        'total_seconds': round(sum(times), 2),\\n        'mean_seconds': round(statistics.mean(times), 3),\\n        'median_seconds': round(statistics.median(times), 3),\\n        'p90_seconds': round(sorted(times)[int(len(times) * 0.9)], 3),\\n        'max_seconds': round(max(times), 3),\\n        'samples_per_minute': round(len(times) / (sum(times) / 60), 2) if sum(times) > 0 else 0,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef load_violation_report(viol_path):\\n    if not viol_path.exists():\\n        return None\\n    with open(viol_path) as f:\\n        report = json.load(f)\\n    s = report.get('summary', {})\\n    result = {\\n        'ET%': round(s.get('entity_type_violation_rate', 0) * 100, 2),\\n        'RV%': round(s.get('relation_vocabulary_violation_rate', 0) * 100, 2),\\n        'DR%': round(s.get('domain_range_violation_rate', 0) * 100, 2),\\n        'CF%': round(s.get('cross_field_consistency_violation_rate', 0) * 100, 2),\\n        'total_violation_rate%': round(s.get('total_violation_rate', 0) * 100, 2),\\n    }\\n    quality = report.get('quality', {})\\n    if quality:\\n        ef = quality.get('entity_f1', {})\\n        rf = quality.get('relation_f1', {})\\n        if ef:\\n            result['entity_f1'] = ef.get('f1', 0)\\n        if rf:\\n            result['relation_f1'] = rf.get('f1', 0)\\n    return result\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument('--results_dir', required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument('--output_path', default=None)\\n    args =   info = parse_filename(jsonl_path.name)\\n        if not info:\\n            continue\\n        records = []\\n        with open(jsonl_path) as f:\\n            for line in f:\\n                line = line.strip()\\n                if line:\\n                    records.append(json.loads(line))\\n        latency = analyze_latency(records)\\n        viol_name = jsonl_path.stem + violations\\n        configs.append(entry)\\n\\n    configs.sort(key=lambda x: (x['dataset'], x['model'], x['beam'], x['mode']))\\n\\n    print(f\\\"{'Model':<8} {'Dataset':<8} {'Beam':>4} {'RelF1':>6}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"-\\\" * 120)\\n\\n    for c in configs:\\n        lat = c.get('latency', {})\\n        vio = c.get('violations', {})\\n        print(f\\\"{c['model']:<8} f\\\"{vio.get('relation_f1', '-'):>6}\\\")\\n\\n    summary = {'configs': configs, 'total_configs': len(configs)}\\n    output_path = args.output_path or str(results_dir / 'd066_summary.json')\\n then\n        local n_lines=$(wc -l < \"$out_file\")\n        if [ \"$n_lines\" -ge $MAX_DOCS ]; then\n            echo \"[$(date)] SKIP ${out_name} (already complete: ${n_lines} lines)\"\n            return 0\n        fi\n    fi\n\n    echo \"[$(date)] START ${out_name} on GPU0\"\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python run_constrained_extraction.py \\\n        --mode \"$mode\" \\\n        --dataset docred \\\n        --model_path \"$QWEN_MODEL\" \\\n        --data_path \"$DATA_PATH\" \\\n        --constraints_path \"$CONSTRAINTS_PATH\" \\\n        --output_path \"$out_file\" \\\n        --device cuda:0 \\\n        --max_new_tokens $MAX_NEW_TOKENS \\\n        --max_docs $MAX_DOCS \\\n        --seed 42 \\\n        --decoding_strategy beam \\\n        --num_beams \"$beam\" \\\n        --skip_on_error\n    echo \"[$(date)] DONE ${out_name} ($(wc -l < \"$out_file\") lines)\"\n}\n\n# Run 4 configs sequentially: k=2 unc, k=2 con, k=8 unc, k=8 con\necho \"[$(date)] === Starting d066 Qwen DocRED 200-doc Beam Sweep ===\"\nrun_one 2 unconstrained\nrun_one 2 full_s1\nrun_one 8 unconstrained\nrun_one 8 full_s1\n\necho \"[$(date)] === All 4 configs complete ===\"\n\n# Violation checks\necho \"[$(date)] Running violation checks...\"\nfor beam in 2 8; then\n            python violation_checker_docred.py \\\n                --input_path \"$in_file\" \\\n                --constraints_path \"$CONSTRAINTS_PATH\" \\\n                --output_path \"$viol_file\" 2>/dev/null && \\\n                echo \"[$(date)] Violation check done: ${out_name}\" || \\\n                echo \"[$(date)] Violation check FAILED: ${out_name}\"\n        fi\n    done\ndone\n\n# Evaluation\necho \"[$(date)] Running eval_d064.py for each config...\"\nfor beam in 2 8;
